[ASSISTANT]
`target_delay=1000` is worse (976k), so default 500 stays. The bottleneck is the `compressor_4to2`'s serial cout/cin chain (20-bit horizontal ripple). **v3:** replace the CSA tree with a pure recursive 3:2-compressor tree (no horizontal chains). I'll modify design_v2.v in place (its passing score 944460 is already recorded):


  -> edit_file(filename='design_v2.v', old_string='module comp_tree #(', new_string="// Pure 3:2-compressor reduction tree (no horizontal carry chains).\n// All values are kept mod 2^WIDTH_I, matching the original csa_tree.\nmodule compress_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 20\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] rows_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NG = N/3;\n    localparam int unsigned NR = N - 3*NG;\n    localparam int unsigned M  = 2*NG + NR;\n\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin\n            assign sum_o = rows_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if (N == 2) begin\n            assign sum_o = rows_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = rows_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else begin\n            logic [M*WIDTH_I-1:0] rows_m;\n            genvar g;\n            for (g = 0; g < NG; g++) begin : g_comp\n                compressor_3to2 #(\n                    .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),\n                    .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_I)\n                ) u_comp (\n                    .operands_i(rows_i[(3*g)*WIDTH_I +: 3*WIDTH_I]),\n                    .sum_o(rows_m[(2*g)*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I]),\n                    .carry_o(rows_m[(2*g+1)*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I])\n                );\n            end\n            genvar r;\n            for (r = 0; r < NR; r++) begin : g_pass\n                assign rows_m[(2*NG + r)*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I] = rows_i[(3*NG + r)*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n            end\n            compress_tree #(\n                .N(M),\n                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I)\n            ) u_recurse (\n                .rows_i(rows_m),\n                .sum_o(sum_o),\n                .carry_o(carry_o)\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(")

module posit_encoder #(
    parameter int unsigned n = 16,
    parameter int unsigned es = 1,
    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),
    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,
    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3
) (
    input logic sign_i,
    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,
    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,

    output logic [n-1:0] result_o
);
    logic input_not_zero;
    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];

    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;
    logic signed [es:0] exp;

    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];
    if(es==0) begin
        assign exp = 0;
    end
    else begin
        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];
    end

    logic sign_k;
    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;
    logic [n-2:0] regime;

    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];
    assign rg_const = 1;
    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;

    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;

    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);

    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;
    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;

    if(es==0) begin
        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};
    end
    else begin
        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};
    end

    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;
    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);
    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;

    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));

    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;

    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;
    barrel_shifter #(
        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),
        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),
        .MODE(1'b1)
    ) u_barrel_shifter (
        .operand_i(value_before_shift),
        .shift_amount(shift_amount),
        .result_o(value_after_shift)
    );

    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;
    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;

    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];

    logic round_bit;
    logic sticky_bit;
    logic round_value;
    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;

    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];
    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];
    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);
    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;

    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;
    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};
    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;

endmodule

module pdpu_top_pipelined #(
    parameter int unsigned N = 16,
    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,
    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,
    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,
    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,
    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14
)(
    input logic clk,
    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,
    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,
    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,
    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o
);
    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;
    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;
    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;
    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;
    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O);
    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);
    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);
    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);
    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;

    // ********************
    // Stage 1: posit decoding
    // ********************
    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;
    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;
    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_norm_a, mants_norm_b;

    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding
            posit_decoder #(
                .n(n_i),
                .es(es_i)
            )ua_posit_decoder(
                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),
                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),
                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])
            );

            posit_decoder #(
                .n(n_i),
                .es(es_i)
            )ub_posit_decoder(
                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),
                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),
                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])
            );
        end
    endgenerate

    logic sign_acc;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;

    posit_decoder #(
        .n(n_o),
        .es(es_o)
    )uc_posit_decoder(
        .operand_i(acc),
        .sign_o(sign_acc),
        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),
        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)
    );

    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;
    logic [N:0] signs;

    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;
    assign signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};

    // product exponents
    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;
    generate
        genvar m;
        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin
            assign rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) + $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);
        end
    endgenerate

    // pairwise max of adjacent product exponents (associative max: value-identical)
    logic signed [(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_pmax;
    generate
        genvar pm;
        for(pm=0;pm<N/2;pm++) begin
            logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] s0, s1;
            assign s0 = rg_exp_c[(2*pm)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];
            assign s1 = rg_exp_c[(2*pm+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];
            assign rg_exp_pmax[(pm)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = (s0 > s1) ? s0 : s1;
        end
    endgenerate

    // quad max: max of 4 adjacent product exponents (2 comparator levels pre-registered)
    logic signed [(N/4)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_qmax;
    generate
        genvar qm;
        for(qm=0;qm<N/4;qm++) begin

            assign rg_exp_qmax[(qm)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = (q0 > q1) ? q0 : q1;
        end
    endgenerate

    // popcount of signs, balanced tree (signs are just wires/XORs of input bits,
    // so this can run in stage 1 in parallel with decoding)

    logic [CARRY_WIDTH-1:0] pc2_0, pc2_1, pc2_2, pc2_3, pc2_4, pc2_5, pc2_6, pc2_7;
    logic [CARRY_WIDTH-1:0] pc4_0, pc4_1, pc4_2, pc4_3;

    assign pc2_2 = {4'd0,signs[4]}  + {4'd0,signs[5]};
    assign pc2_3 = {4'd0,signs[6]}  + {4'd0,signs[7]};

    // Pipeline 1

    logic signed [(N/4)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_qmax;
    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;
    logic [CARRY_WIDTH-1:0] pipe1_signs_pop;

    `FFNR(pipe1_signs,signs,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_c,rg_exp_c,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_qmax,rg_exp_qmax,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_a,mants_norm_a,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_b,mants_norm_b,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_acc,rg_exp_acc,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mant_norm_acc,mant_norm_acc,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_signs_pop,signs_pop,clk)

    // ********************
    // Stage 2: mantissa multiplication + max exponent
    // ********************
    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mants_norm_c;
    generate
        genvar j;
        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin: multiplication
            assign mants_norm_c[(j)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] =
                pipe1_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *
                pipe1_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];
        end
    endgenerate

    generate

        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin
            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];
        end
    endgenerate
    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);

    // max tree over the pairwise-max-reduced items: 9 entries instead of 17

    assign rg_exp_items_reduced[(N/2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] = pipe1_rg_exp_qmax;

    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;
    comp_tree #(

        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)

    // exponent differences + shift-amount clamping in stage 2
    logic [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_diff;
    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;
    generate
        genvar z;
        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin

            assign diff = $unsigned(rg_exp_max - rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);
            if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin
                assign shift_amount[(z)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = (|diff[EXP_WIDTH:SHIFT_WIDTH]) ? SHIFT_WIDTH'(ALIGN_WIDTH) : diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];
            end
            else begin
                assign shift_amount[(z)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_max,rg_exp_max,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_shift_amount,shift_amount,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_mants_norm_c,mants_norm_c,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_mant_norm_acc,pipe1_mant_norm_acc,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_signs_pop,pipe1_signs_pop,clk)

    // ********************
    // Stage 3: alignment (right shifts)
    // ********************
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_shifted;

    generate
        genvar k;
        if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MUL_WIDTH) begin: fixed_shift
            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin
                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);
            end
        end
        else begin: fixed_shift
            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin
                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] >> (MUL_WIDTH-ALIGN_WIDTH);
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+2) begin
        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);
    end
    else begin
        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mant_norm_acc >> (MANT_WIDTH_O+2-ALIGN_WIDTH);
    end

    // Pre-invert the magnitude of negative lanes BEFORE the alignment shift
    // (~P >> d with 0-fill == ~(P >> d); the +1 terms of all two's complements
    // are folded into the popcount row below). The alignment is then an
    // arithmetic right shift, whose sign fill restores the exact two's
    // complement value bit-for-bit.
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_signed;
    generate
        genvar ps;
        for(ps=0;ps<N+1;ps++) begin
            assign product_signed[(ps)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =
                product[(ps)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[ps]}};
        end
    endgenerate

    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa_shifted;
    // Only the low ALIGN_WIDTH bits need shifting; the upper bits are pure
    // sign fill. Express the fill-shift as a logical shift of a sign-prefixed
    // value so the synthesizer infers a narrow (ALIGN_WIDTH) barrel shifter.
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_signed_shifted;
    generate

        if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin

                assign mantissa_shifted[(s2)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =

            end
        end
        else begin
            for(s2=0;s2<N+1;s2++) begin
                assign product_signed_shifted[(s2)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =
                    ({ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[s2]}}, product_signed[(s2)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] } >> pipe2_shift_amount[(s2)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]);
                assign mantissa_shifted[(s2)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =

            end
        end
    endgenerate

    // Summation rows: shifted two's-complement values + the popcount row that
    // absorbs all the two's-complement +1 terms.
    logic [(N+2)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa_all;
    generate

        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin

    endgenerate

    logic [MID_ROWS*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rows_mid;
    compress_partial #(
        .N(N+2),
        .M(MID_ROWS),
        .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1)

    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)

    // ********************

        .N(MID_ROWS),
        .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1)
    ) u_csa_tree (
        .rows_i(pipe3_rows_mid),
        .sum_o(csa_sum),
        .carry_o(csa_carry)
    );

    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;

    // ********************
    // Pipeline 4
    // ********************
    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe4_sum_result;

    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_result,sum_result,clk)

    logic final_sign;

    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];
    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe4_sum_result+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];

    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;
    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;

    mantissa_norm #(
        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),
        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),
        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)
    ) u_mantissa_norm (
        .operand_i(sum_c),
        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),
        .result_o(sum_norm)
    );
    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;

    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;

    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin
        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;
        logic sticky_bit;
        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];
        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;
        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};
    end
    else begin
        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);
    end

    // ********************
    // Pipeline 5
    // ********************
    logic pipe5_final_sign;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;

[ASSISTANT]
## Session Summary

**Best result: `design_v2.v` — area_delay_product 731,465** (baseline `starting_point.v`: 968,045 → **24.4% reduction**). All 1,999,992 checks pass, bit-exact vs the combinational oracle.

### What worked (in order of impact)
1. **Pairwise-max exponent pre-reduction in stage 1** (17→9 items into the comparator tree): 876k→744k. Associativity of max made this free and bit-exact; it cut a full comparator level off the critical stage-2 path.
2. **Replacing the original `compressor_4to2`-based CSA tree with a pure recursive 3:2-compressor tree** (`compress_tree`/`compress_partial` modules): 944k→888k. The original 4:2 compressor had a horizontal `cout→cin` ripple across all bits — a huge hidden chain.
3. **Two's-complement +1 folding**: `-P = ~P + 1` rewritten so all +1 terms accumulate as one extra `popcount(signs)` CSA row. This removed 17 incrementers from the critical path and let me register 14-bit aligned products instead of 20-bit two's-complemented values.
4. **Pipeline re-cutting**: moved alignment + sign-XOR + two compression levels (18→6 rows) into stage 3, registering only 120 flops instead of 340; stage 4 does 3 compression levels + final add.
5. **Pre-inversion before alignment shift**: `~P` applied before the barrel shifter, then an arithmetic right shift restores exact two's complement values (identity `~(p>>d)+1 ≡ (signext(~p)>>>d)+1` mod 2^W). Removed the XOR chain from the path tail: 744k→735k.
6. **Plain `*` for the 4×4 mantissa multipliers** with only 8-bit products registered (vs 2×8-bit booth sum+carry pairs).

### What failed / regressed
- **MID_ROWS ≠ 6** (tried 4, 5, 9, 12) — all worse than 6; the tool found the sweet spot at 6 registered rows.
- **Moving diff/clamp into stage 2** — stacking max-tree + 17 subtractors made stage 2 the bottleneck (854k); reverted.
- **`target_delay=1000`** — slightly worse than default 500; no need to tune it.
- One edit (`ALIGN_WIDTH[...]` select truncation + inconsistent file state during a bisect) caused a functional failure that took a couple of steps to untangle — the fix was to restore the exact last-passing structure wholesale.

### Lessons learned
- Register the *narrowest* intermediate representation at each pipeline cut; shift the flop boundary so each stage carries roughly equal logic depth.
- Hunt for hidden carry chains (the 4:2 compressor's `cout` ripple was the single biggest original-design flaw).
- Algebraic rewrites that are identities (popcount +1 folding, pre-inversion + arithmetic shift) are safe, big-win optimizations for dot-product paths.
- Evaluate after every change and revert immediately on regression — the passing-cost history made recovery painless.
- `design_v2.v` is the final deliverable; `starting_point.v` remains untouched for reference.
